232	class MiniCPMBackendMetadata:
233	    """Metadata to be init once in the model forward pass,
234	    each layer's forward pass can reuse the metadata.
235	
236	    For each init metadata function, we will try set up them in below order
237	    """
238	
239	    # Sequence lengths for the forward batch
240	    cache_seqlens_int32: torch.Tensor = None
241	    # Maximum sequence length for query
242	    max_seq_len_q: int = 1
243	    # Maximum sequence length for key
244	    max_seq_len_k: int = 0
245	    # Cumulative sequence lengths for query
246	    cu_seqlens_q: torch.Tensor = None
247	    # Cumulative sequence lengths for key
248	    cu_seqlens_k: torch.Tensor = None
249	
250	    # Window size (typically used by Gemma)
251	    window_size: tuple = (-1, -1)
252	    # Page table, the index of KV Cache Tables/Blocks
253	    page_table: torch.Tensor = None
254	    # Page table for Sliding Window Attention
255	    swa_page_table: torch.Tensor = None
256	    total_q: int = -1  # use for max_pooling_1d_varlen
257	
258	    # Flashinfer-specific metadata (pre-converted to avoid graph capture issues)
259	    flashinfer_kv_indptr: torch.Tensor = None
260	    flashinfer_kv_indices: torch.Tensor = None
261	    flashinfer_kv_last_page_len: torch.Tensor = None
262	
263	    # Stage1 optimization metadata
264	    cu_seqlens_q_adjusted: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None
265	    max_seqlen_q_adjusted: Optional[int] = None
266	    cache_seqlens_int32_stage1: torch.Tensor = None
267	
268	    # TARGET_VERIFY: FlashInfer prefill wrapper (CUDA graph compatible)
269	    verify_prefill_wrapper: object = None
270	    # TARGET_VERIFY tree-shape: FlashInfer prefill wrapper planned with custom_mask
271	    # （DDTree 等 tree spec 路径专用，跟 verify_prefill_wrapper 互斥；前者 causal=True，
272	    # 后者 plan(custom_mask=tree_mask)）
273	    verify_tree_mask_wrapper: object = None
274	
275	
276	# Copied from:
277	# https://github.com/houseroad/vllm/blob/4e45bfcaf928bdb9bd952b4ac922a3c205589ae8/vllm/v1/attention/backends/flash_attn.py
278	#
279	# Take in `query_start_loc_np` and `seq_lens_np` and break the sequences into
280	# local attention blocks, where each block is passed to the attention kernel
281	# as an independent local ("virtual") batch item.
282	#
283	# For example, if are performing a chunked prefill a batch of 3 sequences:
284	#   q_seqlens  = [4, 10, 5]
285	#   kv_seqlens = [6, 17, 9]
286	# Then normally for regular attention we would compute with an attention mask
287	#  for batch idx 0 (q_seqlens = 4, kv_seqlens = 6) like:
288	#   batch idx: 0 (q_seqlens = 4, kv_seqlens = 6)
289	#        k_toks >   0 1 2 3 4 5
290	#        q_toks v  _____________
291	#               0 | 1 1 1
292	#               1 | 1 1 1 1
293	#               2 | 1 1 1 1 1
294	#               3 | 1 1 1 1 1 1
295	#
296	# for local attention (with attn_chunk_size = 4) we would compute with an
297	#  attention mask like:
298	#   batch idx: 0  (q_seqlens = 4, kv_seqlens = 6, attn_chunk_size = 4)
299	#        k_toks >   0 1 2 3 4 5
300	#        q_toks v  _____________
301	#               0 | 1 1 1
302	#               1 | 1 1 1 1
303	#               2 |         1
304	#               3 |         1 1
305	#
306	# We can simulate this mask using standard flash-attention by breaking the
307	#  sequences into local ("virtual") batches, where each local batch item is a
308	#  local attention block, so in this case batch idx 0 would be broken up into:
309	#
310	#   local-batch idx: 0 (q_seqlens = 2, kv_seqlens = 4)  (batch 0)
311	#        k_toks >   0 1 2 3
312	#        q_toks v  _____________
313	#               0 | 1 1 1
314	#               1 | 1 1 1 1
315	#   local-batch idx: 1 (q_seqlens = 2, kv_seqlens = 2) (batch 0)
316	#        k_toks >   4 5
317	#        q_toks v  _____________
318	#               2 | 1
319	#               3 | 1 1
320	#
321	# e.g. if we have:
322	#   attn_chunk_size = 4
323	#   query_start_loc_np = [0, 4, 14, 19] (q_seqlens = [4, 10, 5])
324	# Then this function would return:
325	#                           __b0__  ______b1______  __b2__ < orig batch indices
326	#   q_seqlens_local    = [   2,  2,  1,  4,  4,  1,  4,  1]
327	#   cu_seqlens_q_local = [0, 4,  6, 10, 14, 18, 19, 23, 24]
328	#   seqlens_k_local    = [   4,  2,  4,  4,  4,  1,  4,  1]
329	#   block_table_local  : shape[local_virtual_batches, pages_per_local_batch]
330	def cdiv(a: int, b: int) -> int:
331	    """Ceiling division."""
332	    return -(a // -b)
333

335	class MiniCPMSparseBackend(AttentionBackend):
336	    """FlashAttention backend implementation.
337	
338	    Note about the init:
339	    - If no spec decoding
340	        - FlashAttentionBackend will be init once when the server starts.
341	    - If spec decoding
342	        - FlashAttentionBackend will be init once for the target worker
343	        - FlashAttentionMultiStepBackend will be once for the draft worker
344	            - It will spawn num_steps FlashAttentionBackend for the draft worker
345	
346	    Note about CUDA Graph:
347	    - We only support CUDA Graph for Decode (Normal Decode and Draft Decode) and Target Verify.
348	    - We don't support CUDA Graph for Extend and Draft Extend.
349	    - When server init, init_cuda_graph_state will be called first and then init_cuda_graph_capture will be called.
350	    - For each forward batch, init_replay_cuda_graph will be called first and then replay the graph.
351	    """
352	
353	    def __init__(
354	        self,
355	        model_runner: ModelRunner,
356	        skip_prefill: bool = False,
357	        fa_impl_ver=3,
358	    ):
359	
360	        self.forward_metadata: MiniCPMBackendMetadata = None
361	        self.max_context_len = model_runner.model_config.context_len
362	        self.device = model_runner.device
363	        self.enable_cuda_graph = not get_global_server_args().disable_cuda_graph
364	        self.decode_cuda_graph_metadata = {}
365	        self.[REDACTED]
366	        self.req_to_sparse_k1_token = (
367	            model_runner.req_to_token_pool.req_to_sparse_k1_token
368	        )
369	        self.req_to_sparse_k2_token = (
370	            model_runner.req_to_token_pool.req_to_sparse_k2_token
371	        )
372	        self.kv_cache_dtype = model_runner.kv_cache_dtype
373	        self.kv_cache_dtype_str = model_runner.server_args.kv_cache_dtype
374	        self.params_dtype = model_runner.dtype
375	        self.page_size = model_runner.page_size
376	        # MiniCPM does not support local attention
377	        self.use_mla = False
378	        self.attention_chunk_size = None
379	        self.is_encoder_decoder = False
380	        self.skip_prefill = skip_prefill
381	        tp_size = get_tensor_model_parallel_world_size()
382	        self.num_kv_heads = model_runner.model_config.num_key_value_heads // tp_size
383	
384	        self.fa_impl_ver = fa_impl_ver
385	
386	        # For each layer, the sliding_window_size can be different. This is only used for preparing SWA metadata.
387	        # We use `layer.sliding_window_size` to decide whether to use SWA for each layer.
388	        self.sliding_window_size = model_runner.sliding_window_size
389	        self.has_swa = (
390	            self.sliding_window_size is not None and self.sliding_window_size > -1
391	        )
392	
393	        # If num_splits == 0, we use a heuristic to automatically determine the number of splits.
394	        # We set nums splits to 1 if deterministic inference is enabled.
395	        # See https://thinkingmachines.ai/blog/defeating-nondeterminism-in-llm-inference/ for more details.
396	        self.num_splits_prefill = 0   # prefill keeps auto-split for perf
397	        self.num_splits_decode = 1    # decode+verify deterministic
398	
399	        # Sparse attention configuration (required for MiniCPM)
400	        hf_config = getattr(model_runner.model_config, "hf_config", None)
401	        self.has_sparse_attention = hf_config is not None and getattr(
402	            hf_config, "has_sparse_attention", False
403	        )
404	
405	        # MiniCPM must have sparse attention enabled
406	        if not self.has_sparse_attention:
407	            raise ValueError(
408	                "MiniCPM model must have sparse attention enabled. "
409	                "Please ensure the model config has 'has_sparse_attention=True'."
410	            )
411	
412	        self.kernel_size = hf_config.sparse_kernel_size
413	        self.kernel_stride = hf_config.sparse_kernel_stride
414	        self.init_blocks = hf_config.sparse_init_blocks
415	        self.block_size = hf_config.sparse_block_size
416	        self.window_size = hf_config.sparse_window_size
417	        self.dense_as_sparse = model_runner.server_args.dense_as_sparse
418	        self.dense_len = 0 if self.dense_as_sparse else hf_config.sparse_dense_len
419	        self.config_dense_len = hf_config.sparse_dense_len
420	        topk = hf_config.sparse_topk
421	        self.use_nope = hf_config.sparse_use_nope
422	        self.local_blocks = self.window_size // self.block_size  # local_blocks
423	        self.sparse_topk = topk + (self.window_size // self.block_size)
424	        self.num_sparse_topk_tokens = self.block_size * self.sparse_topk
425	
426	        # Head group number derived from model configuration
427	        self.head_dim = model_runner.model_config.head_dim
428	        self.head_group_num = model_runner.model_config.num_key_value_heads
429	        self.heads_per_group = (
430	            model_runner.model_config.num_attention_heads // self.head_group_num
431	        )
432	        self.k1_kernel_size = self.kernel_size
433	        self.k1_kernel_stride = self.kernel_stride
434	        self.k2_kernel_size = self.kernel_size * 4
435	        self.k2_kernel_stride = self.kernel_stride * 4
436	
437	        self.fuse_topk = model_runner.server_args.fuse_topk
438	        self.split_stage1 = model_runner.server_args.split_stage1
439	
440	
441	        max_cache_len = self.max_context_len
442	        pooled_k_len = (max_cache_len + self.block_size - 1) // self.block_size
443	
444	        output_topk = min(self.sparse_topk, pooled_k_len)
445	
446	        # For the kernel, we need power of 2 topk
447	        topk_power2 = tilelang.math.next_power_of_2(output_topk)
448	        kernel_topk = min(topk_power2, pooled_k_len)
449	        # Make sure it's still power of 2
450	        if kernel_topk != tilelang.math.next_power_of_2(kernel_topk):
451	            kernel_topk = tilelang.math.next_power_of_2(kernel_topk) // 2
452	        kernel_topk = max(8, kernel_topk)
453	        dtype_str = "float16" if self.params_dtype == torch.float16 else "bfloat16"
454	        self.decode_fused_kernels = {}
455	        self.prefill_fused_kernels = {}
456	        bucketed_pooled_k_len = _bucket_size(pooled_k_len)
457	
458	        pooling_block_stride = self.block_size // self.kernel_stride  # = 64 // 16 = 4
459	        pooling_pad_len = self.kernel_size // self.kernel_stride - 1  # = 32 // 16 - 1 = 1
460	        pooling_num_offs = self.kernel_size // self.kernel_stride + self.block_size // self.kernel_stride - 1
461

463	            print("jit start...")

71	class ForwardMode(IntEnum):
72	    # Extend a sequence. The KV cache of the beginning part of the sequence is already computed (e.g., system prompt).
73	    # It is also called "prefill" in common terminology.
74	    EXTEND = auto()
75	    # Decode one token.
76	    DECODE = auto()
77	    # Contains both EXTEND and DECODE when doing chunked prefill.
78	    MIXED = auto()
79	    # No sequence to forward. For data parallel attention, some workers will be IDLE if no sequence are allocated.
80	    IDLE = auto()
81	
82	    # Used in speculative decoding: verify a batch in the target model.
83	    TARGET_VERIFY = auto()
84	    # Used in speculative decoding: extend a batch in the draft model.
85	    DRAFT_EXTEND = auto()
86	
87	    DRAFT_EXTEND_V2 = auto()
88	
89	    # Used in disaggregated decode worker
90	    # Represent a batch of requests having their KV cache ready to start decoding
91	    PREBUILT = auto()
92	
93	    # Split Prefill for PD multiplexing
94	    SPLIT_PREFILL = auto()
95	
96	    # Used in diffusion LLM inference
97	    DLLM_EXTEND = auto()
98	
99	    def is_prefill(self):
100	        return self.is_extend()
101	
102	    def is_extend(self, include_draft_extend_v2: bool = False):
103	        return (
104	            self == ForwardMode.EXTEND
105	            or self == ForwardMode.MIXED
106	            or self == ForwardMode.DRAFT_EXTEND
107	            or (include_draft_extend_v2 and self == ForwardMode.DRAFT_EXTEND_V2)
108	            or self == ForwardMode.TARGET_VERIFY
109	            or self == ForwardMode.SPLIT_PREFILL
110	            or self == ForwardMode.DLLM_EXTEND
111	        )
112	
113	    def is_context_parallel_extend(self, include_draft_extend_v2: bool = False):
114	        return (
115	            self == ForwardMode.EXTEND
116	            or self == ForwardMode.MIXED
117	            or (
118	                self == ForwardMode.DRAFT_EXTEND_V2
119	                if include_draft_extend_v2
120	                else False
121	            )
122	        )
123	
124	    def is_decode(self):
125	        return self == ForwardMode.DECODE
126	
127	    def is_mixed(self):
128	        return self == ForwardMode.MIXED
129	
130	    def is_idle(self):
131	        return self == ForwardMode.IDLE
132	
133	    def is_decode_or_idle(self):
134	        return self == ForwardMode.DECODE or self == ForwardMode.IDLE
135	
136	    def is_target_verify(self):
137	        return self == ForwardMode.TARGET_VERIFY
138	
139	    def is_draft_extend(self, include_v2: bool = False):
140	        return self == ForwardMode.DRAFT_EXTEND or (
141	            include_v2 and self == ForwardMode.DRAFT_EXTEND_V2
142	        )
143	
144	    def is_draft_extend_v2(self):
145	        # For fixed shape logits output in eagle v2 worker
146	        return self == ForwardMode.DRAFT_EXTEND_V2
147	
148	    def is_extend_or_draft_extend_or_mixed(self, include_draft_extend_v2: bool = False):
149	        return (
150	            self == ForwardMode.EXTEND
151	            or self == ForwardMode.DRAFT_EXTEND
152	            or self == ForwardMode.MIXED
153	            or self == ForwardMode.SPLIT_PREFILL
154	            or (include_draft_extend_v2 and self == ForwardMode.DRAFT_EXTEND_V2)
155	        )
156

164

167	
168	    def is_split_prefill(self):

170

175	            and not self.is_draft_extend()
176	        )

180	
181	    def is_dllm_extend(self):

1580	        self,
1581	        forward_batch: ForwardBatch,
1582	        base_metadata: FlashAttentionMetadata,
1583	        req_to_sparse_k1_token: torch.Tensor,
1584	        req_to_sparse_k2_token: torch.Tensor,

1586	        k1_kernel_stride: int,
1587	        k2_kernel_size: int,
1588	        k2_kernel_stride: int,
1589	        cu_seqlens_q: torch.Tensor,
1590	    ) -> dict[str, CompressionLevelMetadata]:
1591	        """Build k1/k2 compression metadata.
1592	
1593	        This method computes all k1/k2 compressed cache metadata needed for
1594	        sparse attention by calling _compute_single_compression_metadata for each level.
1595	
1596	        Args:
1597	            forward_batch: The forward batch to analyze
1598	            base_metadata: Base metadata with cu_seqlens_q, cu_seqlens_k
1599	            req_to_sparse_k1_token: Mapping from request pool to sparse k1 tokens
1600	            req_to_sparse_k2_token: Mapping from request pool to sparse k2 tokens
1601	            k1_kernel_size: Kernel size for k1 compression
1602	            k1_kernel_stride: Kernel stride for k1 compression
1603	            k2_kernel_size: Kernel size for k2 compression
1604	            k2_kernel_stride: Kernel stride for k2 compression
1605	            cu_seqlens_q: Cumulative query sequence lengths
1606	
1607	        Returns:
1608	            Dictionary with 'k1' and 'k2' keys containing CompressionLevelMetadata
1609	        """
1610	        # Compute k1 metadata
1611	        k1_dict = self._compute_single_compression_metadata(
1612	            forward_batch,
1613	            base_metadata,
1614	            req_to_sparse_k1_token,
1615	            k1_kernel_size,
1616	            k1_kernel_stride,
1617	            cu_seqlens_q,
1618	        )
1619	
1620	        # Compute k2 metadata
1621	        k2_dict = self._compute_single_compression_metadata(

1657	    def build_sparse_prefill_metadata(
1658	        self,
1659	        forward_batch: ForwardBatch,
1660	        base_metadata: FlashAttentionMetadata,
1661	        sparse_bs_list: list[int],
1662	        head_group_num: int,
1663	        dense_len: int,
1664	        sparse_topk: int,
1665	        block_size: int,
1666	        cu_seqlens_q: torch.Tensor,
1667	        sparse_page_table_dtype: torch.dtype,
1668	        sparse_page_table_device: torch.device,
1669	    ) -> dict:
1670	        """Build sparse prefill metadata.
1671	
1672	        This method handles the complex page table and batch mapping logic
1673	        for sparse prefill mode.
1674	
1675	        Args:
1676	            forward_batch: The forward batch to analyze
1677	            base_metadata: Base metadata with cu_seqlens_q
1678	            sparse_bs_list: List of sparse batch indices
1679	            head_group_num: Number of head groups
1680	            dense_len: Dense length threshold for sparse activation
1681	            sparse_topk: Top-K value for sparse attention
1682	            block_size: Block size for sparse attention
1683	            cu_seqlens_q: Cumulative query sequence lengths
1684	            sparse_page_table_dtype: Data type for sparse page table
1685	            sparse_page_table_device: Device for sparse page table
1686	
1687	        Returns:
1688	            Dictionary with prefill metadata
1689	        """
1690	        bs = forward_batch.batch_size
1691	        all_sparse_prefill = (
1692	            _SGLANG_MINICPM_ALL_SPARSE_PREFILL_METADATA
1693	            and len(sparse_bs_list) == bs
1694	            and all(int(forward_batch.seq_lens_cpu[i]) >= dense_len for i in range(bs))
1695	        )
1696	
1697	        if all_sparse_prefill:
1698	            max_sparse_cache_len = sparse_topk * block_size
1699	            sparse_page_table_bs = 0
1700	            old_bs_to_new_bs_range = [0 for _ in range(bs + 1)]
1701	            for i in range(bs):
1702	                sparse_page_table_bs += (
1703	                    int(forward_batch.extend_seq_lens_cpu[i]) * head_group_num
1704	                )
1705	                old_bs_to_new_bs_range[i + 1] = sparse_page_table_bs
1706	
1707	            sparse_page_table = torch.empty(
1708	                (sparse_page_table_bs, max_sparse_cache_len),
1709	                dtype=sparse_page_table_dtype,
1710	                device=sparse_page_table_device,
1711	            )
1712	            sparse_cu_seqlens_q_cpu = torch.arange(
1713	                sparse_page_table_bs + 1,
1714	                dtype=cu_seqlens_q.dtype,
1715	                device="cpu",
1716	            )
1717	
1718	            return {
1719	                "sparse_page_table": sparse_page_table,
1720	                "sparse_cu_seqlens_q_cpu": sparse_cu_seqlens_q_cpu,
1721	                "sparse_cu_seqlens_q": sparse_cu_seqlens_q_cpu.to(
1722	                    device=cu_seqlens_q.device
1723	                ),
1724	                "old_bs_to_new_bs_range": old_bs_to_new_bs_range,
1725	                "sparse_max_seq_len_q": 1,
1726	                "sparse_idx": slice(None),
1727	            }
1728	
1729	        max_sparse_cache_len = -1
1730	        sparse_page_table_bs = 0
1731	        old_bs_to_new_bs_range = [0 for _ in range(bs + 1)]
1732	        sparse_max_seq_len_q = 1
1733	
1734	        for i in range(bs):
1735	            if forward_batch.seq_lens_cpu[i] >= dense_len:
1736	                max_sparse_cache_len = max(
1737	                    max_sparse_cache_len, sparse_topk * block_size
1738	                )

1888	    def build(
1889	        self, forward_batch: ForwardBatch, base_metadata: object
1890	    ) -> SparseMetadata:
1891	        """Build complete sparse metadata for the forward batch.
1892	
1893	        This method orchestrates the entire metadata building process:
1894	        1. Identify sparse batches
1895	        2. Build sequence lengths
1896	        3. Build token mappings
1897	        4. Get page table from base metadata
1898	
1899	        Args:
1900	            forward_batch: The forward batch to build metadata for
1901	            base_metadata: Base metadata containing page_table, cu_seqlens_q, etc.
1902	
1903	        Returns:
1904	            SparseMetadata instance with all fields populated
1905	        """
1906	        # Identify sparse batches
1907	        analyzer = SparseBatchAnalyzer(self.config)
1908	        sparse_bs_list = analyzer.identify_sparse_batches(forward_batch)
1909	
1910	        # Build sequence lengths
1911	        seqlen_q_sparse_bs, seqlen_k_sparse_bs_tensor = self.build_sequence_lengths(
1912	            base_metadata.cu_seqlens_q, forward_batch.extend_prefix_lens, sparse_bs_list
1913	        )
1914	
1915	        # Build cumulative sequence lengths for sparse batches
1916	        cu_seqlens_q_sparse_bs_cpu = torch.tensor(
1917	            [0] + seqlen_q_sparse_bs,
1918	            dtype=torch.int32,
1919	            device="cpu",
1920	        ).cumsum(dtype=torch.int32, dim=0)
1921	
1922	        # Extract prefix lengths for sparse batches only
1923	        extend_prefix_lens_sparse = forward_batch.extend_prefix_lens_cpu[sparse_bs_list]
1924	
1925	        # Build token mappings
1926	        token_to_bs, token_pos_in_bs = self.build_token_mappings(
1927	            cu_seqlens_q_sparse_bs_cpu, extend_prefix_lens_sparse, seqlen_q_sparse_bs
1928	        )
1929	
1930	        # Get page table
1931	        sparse_page_table = self.build_page_table_base(sparse_bs_list, base_metadata)
1932	
1933	        # Create SparseMetadata instance
1934	        metadata = SparseMetadata(
1935	            sparse_bs_list=sparse_bs_list,
1936	            sparse_cu_seqlens_q_cpu=cu_seqlens_q_sparse_bs_cpu,
1937	            seqlen_q_sparse_bs_tensor=torch.tensor(
1938	                seqlen_q_sparse_bs,
1939	                dtype=torch.int32,
1940	                device=forward_batch.cu_seqlens_q.device,
1941	            ),
1942	            seqlen_k_sparse_bs_tensor=seqlen_k_sparse_bs_tensor,
1943	            token_to_bs=token_to_bs,
1944	            token_pos_in_bs=token_pos_in_bs,
1945	            sparse_page_table=sparse_page_table,
1946	            sparse_max_seq_len_q=max(seqlen_q_sparse_bs),
1947	            sparse_max_seq_len_k=max(seqlen_q_sparse_bs),
1948	        )
1949	
1950	        return metadata

1484	    def _compute_single_compression_metadata(
1485	        self,
1486	        forward_batch: ForwardBatch,
1487	        base_metadata: FlashAttentionMetadata,
1488	        req_to_sparse_token: torch.Tensor,
1489	        kernel_size: int,
1490	        kernel_stride: int,
1491	        cu_seqlens_q: torch.Tensor,
1492	    ) -> dict:
1493	        """Compute compression metadata for a single compression level (k1 or k2).
1494	
1495	        Args:
1496	            forward_batch: The forward batch to analyze
1497	            base_metadata: Base metadata with cu_seqlens_q, cu_seqlens_k
1498	            req_to_sparse_token: Mapping from request pool to sparse tokens
1499	            kernel_size: Kernel size for compression
1500	            kernel_stride: Kernel stride for compression
1501	            cu_seqlens_q: Cumulative query sequence lengths
1502	
1503	        Returns:
1504	            Dictionary with compression metadata fields for this level
1505	        """
1506	        bs = forward_batch.batch_size
1507	        seqlen_cpu = torch.zeros(
1508	            (bs,), dtype=base_metadata.cu_seqlens_q.dtype, device="cpu"
1509	        )
1510	
1511	        for i in range(bs):
1512	            # if forward_batch.forward_mode.is_extend_or_draft_extend_or_mixed():
1513	            #     seqlen_cpu[i] = max(
1514	            #         0,
1515	            #         (forward_batch.extend_seq_lens_cpu[i] - kernel_size)
1516	            #         // kernel_stride
1517	            #         + 1,
1518	            #     )
1519	            # else:
1520	            seqlen_cpu[i] = max(
1521	                0,
1522	                (forward_batch.seq_lens_cpu[i] - kernel_size) // kernel_stride + 1,
1523	            )
1524	
1525	        max_seq_len = seqlen_cpu.max().item()
1526	        cu_seqlens = F.pad(
1527	            torch.cumsum(
1528	                seqlen_cpu.to(device=cu_seqlens_q.device), dim=0, dtype=torch.int32
1529	            ),
1530	            (1, 0),
1531	        )
1532	        token_table = req_to_sparse_token[forward_batch.req_pool_indices]
1533	
1534	        token_nums = base_metadata.cu_seqlens_k[1:] - base_metadata.cu_seqlens_k[:-1]
1535	        input_lens = base_metadata.cu_seqlens_q[1:] - base_metadata.cu_seqlens_q[:-1]
1536	        history_lens = token_nums - input_lens
1537	
1538	        history_compress_token_nums = torch.maximum(
1539	            (history_lens - kernel_size) // kernel_stride + 1,
1540	            torch.zeros(1, device=history_lens.device, dtype=torch.int32),
1541	        )
1542	
1543	        new_token_nums = token_nums - history_compress_token_nums * kernel_stride
1544	
1545	        cu_new_token_nums = F.pad(
1546	            torch.cumsum(new_token_nums, dim=0, dtype=torch.int32), (1, 0)
1547	        )
1548	
1549	        new_compress_token_nums = torch.maximum(
1550	            (new_token_nums - kernel_size) // kernel_stride + 1,
1551	            torch.zeros(1, device=new_token_nums.device, dtype=torch.int32),
1552	        )
1553	
1554	        cu_new_compress_token_nums = F.pad(
1555	            torch.cumsum(new_compress_token_nums, dim=0, dtype=torch.int32), (1, 0)
1556	        )
1557	
1558	        total_compress_token_nums = (
1559	            history_compress_token_nums + new_compress_token_nums
1560	        )
1561	
1562	        cu_total_compress_token_nums = F.pad(
1563	            torch.cumsum(total_compress_token_nums, dim=0, dtype=torch.int32), (1, 0)
1564	        )
1565	
1566	        return {
1567	            "cu_seqlens": cu_seqlens,
1568	            "max_seq_len": max_seq_len,
1569	            "token_table": token_table,
1570	            "history_compress_token_nums": history_compress_token_nums,
1571	            "new_token_nums": new_token_nums,
1572	            "cu_new_token_nums": cu_new_token_nums,
1573	            "new_compress_token_nums": new_compress_token_nums,
1574	            "cu_new_compress_token_nums": cu_new_compress_token_nums,
1575	            "total_compress_token_nums": total_compress_token_nums,
1576	            "cu_total_compress_token_nums": cu_total_compress_token_nums,
1577	        }
1578

13	class HybridAttnBackend(AttentionBackend):
14	    """Support different backends for prefill and decode."""
15	
16	    def __init__(
17	        self,
18	        model_runner: ModelRunner,
19	        prefill_backend: AttentionBackend,
20	        decode_backend: AttentionBackend,
21	    ):
22	        self.model_runner = model_runner
23	        self.prefill_backend = prefill_backend
24	        self.decode_backend = decode_backend
25	        self.data_type = model_runner.kv_cache_dtype
26	
27	    def _select_backend(self, forward_mode: ForwardMode) -> AttentionBackend:
28	        """
29	        Select the appropriate attention backend based on the forward mode.
30	
31	        Args:
32	            forward_mode: The current forward mode indicating the operation type
33	
34	        Returns:
35	            The selected attention backend (prefill or decode)
36	
37	        Note:
38	            - decode_or_idle: Always uses decode backend
39	            - target_verify or draft_extend: Uses decode backend if speculative_attention_mode is "decode", otherwise prefill backend
40	            - prefill: Always uses prefill backend
41	        """
42	        if forward_mode.is_decode_or_idle():

200	def attn_backend_wrapper(runner: "ModelRunner", full_attn_backend: "AttentionBackend"):
201	    """
202	    Wrapper for special models like hybrid GDN, so we don't
203	    need to change the code of the original attention backend.
204	    """
205	    assert not (
206	        runner.hybrid_gdn_config is not None and runner.use_mla_backend
207	    ), "hybrid_gdn can only be used with non-MLA models."
208	
209	    if cfg := runner.mambaish_config:
210	        from sglang.srt.layers.attention.fla.utils import check_environments
211	        from sglang.srt.layers.attention.hybrid_linear_attn_backend import (
212	            GDNAttnBackend,
213	            HybridLinearAttnBackend,
214	            KimiLinearAttnBackend,
215	            Mamba2AttnBackend,
216	            SimpleGLAAttnBackend,
217	        )
218	        from sglang.srt.utils import is_blackwell, is_npu
219	
220	        check_environments()
221	        if runner.hybrid_gdn_config is not None:
222	            if is_blackwell():
223	                assert (
224	                    runner.server_args.attention_backend == "triton"
225	                    or runner.server_args.attention_backend == "trtllm_mha"
226	                ), "triton or trtllm_mha backend are the only supported backends on Blackwell GPUs for hybrid GDN models, use --attention-backend triton or --attention-backend trtllm_mha to specify the backend."
227	            if is_npu():
228	                assert (
229	                    runner.server_args.attention_backend == "ascend"
230	                ), "ascend backend is the only supported backend on NPU for hybrid GDN models, use --attention-backend ascend to specify the backend."
231	            logger.info(f"Using hybrid linear attention backend for hybrid GDN models.")
232	            linear_attn_backend = GDNAttnBackend(runner)
233	        elif runner.mamba2_config is not None:
234	            linear_attn_backend = Mamba2AttnBackend(runner)
235	        elif runner.kimi_linear_config is not None:
236	            linear_attn_backend = KimiLinearAttnBackend(runner)
237	        elif runner.minicpm_hybrid_config is not None:
238	            linear_attn_backend = SimpleGLAAttnBackend(runner)
239	        else:
240	            raise ValueError(
241	                "Expected hybrid GDN, NemotronH, or MiniCPM hybrid models, but got unknown model."
242	            )
243	        full_attn_layers = cfg.full_attention_layer_ids
244	        return HybridLinearAttnBackend(
245	            full_attn_backend, linear_attn_backend, full_attn_layers
246	        )
247	
248	    return full_attn_backend
249

2262	        """Initialize forward metadata for capturing CUDA graph.
2263	
2264	        For FlashInfer sparse mode (tokens > 8192), uses wrapper state persistence pattern:
2265	
2266	        Key Challenge:
2267	        - sparse_page_table is computed DYNAMICALLY during forward() based on query vectors
2268	        - FlashInfer requires kv_indptr/kv_indices converted BEFORE CUDA graph replay
2269	        - Direct conversion fails because converted data becomes stale by replay time
2270	
2271	        Solution: Wrapper State Persistence Pattern
2272	        1. Create wrapper with BUFFER VIEWS (slices of pre-allocated tensors)
2273	        2. Store wrapper references (addresses) - NOT the tensor data
2274	        3. Before each replay: UPDATE UNDERLYING STORAGE at those addresses
2275	        4. Call fast_decode_plan() to sync wrapper's cached pointers/metadata
2276	        5. Wrapper accesses updated storage through same addresses
2277	
2278	        Example:
2279	        - Capture: wrapper = BatchDecodeWrapper(paged_kv_indptr_buffer=full[:bs+1], ...)

2. Key Technical Concepts:

   - InfLLM-v2 sparse attention: compress_k → stage1 block_score → max_pool → topk → stage2 sparse FA

   - SGLang fork architecture: AttentionBackend dispatch, ForwardMode enum, CUDA graph capture/replay

8. Current Work:
   I was deep in re-auditing questions 1 and 2 (InfLLM-v2 stage1/2 in spec verify, and InfLLM-v2 page table reuse in spec verify) per the user's instruction "仔细读代码 仔细调查 仔细思考 1、2 这两个问题 先不测试".

   Key findings confirmed through careful re-read:
   - `MiniCPMSparseBackend` is wrapped by `HybridLinearAttnBackend` (attention_registry.py:200-248)
   - In TARGET_VERIFY, `MiniCPMSparseBackend.init_forward_metadata` sets dense metadata at lines 856-889, then unconditionally calls `update_batch_for_sparse` at line 959
   - `update_batch_for_sparse` checks `is_extend_or_draft_extend_or_mixed()` which excludes TARGET_VERIFY → goes to decode branch (else clause line 640)
   - Decode branch calls `build_sparse_decode_metadata` (line 1805-1886) which only allocates buffers (zero sparse_page_table, indptr calc) — no actual sparse kernel execution
   - `_compute_single_compression_metadata` (line 1484-1577) also only computes metadata, no compress_k kernel
   - `MiniCPMSparseBackend.forward_extend` TARGET_VERIFY branch (line 1389-1466) uses `verify_wrapper.forward(..., causal=True)` (dense FlashInfer) and returns early at line 1466 — never reaches sparse path at line 1468+
   - `dense_len` check at line 1470 is post-return for TARGET_VERIFY, so it's irrelevant
   - CUDA graph replay (line 2865-2971) uses real `seq_lens + dtn` for kv_lens, builds real page indices into `verify_kv_indices` buffer, calls `verify_wrapper.plan(..., causal=True)`
   - `_verify_max_pages_per_seq = (max_context_len + dtn + page_size - 1) // page_size` — buffer sized for FULL max_context_len, so replay can handle long context

   Final confirmed answers:
   - **Question 1 (stage1/2 block scoring redone?)**: NO. spec verify completely bypasses sparse path. `update_batch_for_sparse` only allocates metadata buffers (minor CPU/GPU overhead, no kernel runs). `forward_extend` exits early to dense FlashInfer path.
   - **Question 2 (page table reused?)**: NO. spec verify uses its own dense paged KV table built fresh from `req_to_token_pool` based on real `seq_lens + dtn`. The `sparse_page_table` from prefill is never read by verify path.

   Performance implication confirmed:
   - At long context (e.g., 128K) with dtn=9 or 11, spec verify runs DENSE full causal attention on 8 standard layers, looking at full ~128K KV — this is `dtn × seq_len` attention ops vs normal decode's `1 × sparse_topk × block_size` ops
   - This is a potential efficiency leak at long context but provides mathematical equivalence to dense baseline

1805	    def build_sparse_decode_metadata(
1806	        self,
1807	        forward_batch: ForwardBatch,
1808	        base_metadata: FlashAttentionMetadata,
1809	        head_group_num: int,
1810	        dense_len: int,
1811	        sparse_topk: int,
1812	        block_size: int,
1813	    ) -> dict:
1814	        """Build sparse decode metadata.
1815	
1816	        This method handles sparse attention metadata for decode mode.
1817	
1818	        Args:
1819	            forward_batch: The forward batch to analyze
1820	            base_metadata: Base metadata with cache_seqlens_int32, page_table
1821	            head_group_num: Number of head groups
1822	            dense_len: Dense length threshold
1823	            sparse_topk: Top-K value for sparse attention
1824	            block_size: Block size
1825	
1826	        Returns:
1827	            Dictionary with decode metadata
1828	        """
1829	        bs = forward_batch.batch_size
1830	        cache_seqlens = base_metadata.cache_seqlens_int32
1831	        page_table = base_metadata.page_table
1832	        max_sparse_cache_len = 0
1833	
1834	        sparse_cache_seqlens_cpu = torch.zeros(
1835	            (bs * head_group_num,), dtype=cache_seqlens.dtype, device="cpu"
1836	        )
1837	
1838	        for b in range(bs):
1839	            if forward_batch.seq_lens_cpu[b] >= dense_len:
1840	                if forward_batch.seq_lens_cpu[b] <= sparse_topk * block_size:
1841	                    sparse_cache_len = forward_batch.seq_lens_cpu[b]
1842	                elif cache_seqlens[b] % block_size == 0:
1843	                    sparse_cache_len = sparse_topk * block_size
1844	                else:
1845	                    sparse_cache_len = block_size * (sparse_topk - 1) + (
1846	                        cache_seqlens[b] % block_size
1847	                    )
1848	
1849	                if sparse_cache_len > max_sparse_cache_len:
1850	                    max_sparse_cache_len = sparse_cache_len
1851	
1852	                sparse_cache_seqlens_cpu[2 * b] = sparse_cache_len
1853	                sparse_cache_seqlens_cpu[2 * b + 1] = sparse_cache_len
1854	            else:
1855	                if cache_seqlens[b] > max_sparse_cache_len:
1856	                    max_sparse_cache_len = cache_seqlens[b]
1857	
1858	                sparse_cache_seqlens_cpu[2 * b] = cache_seqlens[b]
1859	                sparse_cache_seqlens_cpu[2 * b + 1] = cache_seqlens[b]
1860	
1861	        sparse_cache_seqlens_int32 = sparse_cache_seqlens_cpu.to(
1862	            device=cache_seqlens.device
1863	        )
1864	        sparse_cu_seqlens_k = F.pad(
1865	            torch.cumsum(sparse_cache_seqlens_int32, dim=0, dtype=torch.int32), (1, 0)
1866	        )
1867	        sparse_cu_seqlens_q = torch.arange(
1868	            0,
1869	            bs * head_group_num + 1,
1870	            dtype=torch.int32,
1871	            device=base_metadata.cu_seqlens_q.device,
1872	        )
1873	        token_to_bs = torch.arange(0, bs, dtype=torch.int32, device="cuda")
1874	        sparse_page_table = torch.zeros(
1875	            (2 * bs, sparse_topk * block_size),
1876	            dtype=page_table.dtype,
1877	            device=page_table.device,
1878	        )
1879	
1880	        return {
1881	            "sparse_cache_seqlens_int32": sparse_cache_seqlens_int32,
1882	            "sparse_cu_seqlens_k": sparse_cu_seqlens_k,
1883	            "sparse_cu_seqlens_q": sparse_cu_seqlens_q,
1884	            "sparse_page_table": sparse_page_table,
1885	            "token_to_bs": token_to_bs,
1886	        }
1887	
1888	    def build(
1889	        self, forward_batch: ForwardBatch, base_metadata: object
1890	    ) -> SparseMetadata:
1891	        """Build complete sparse metadata for the forward batch.
1892	
1893	        This method orchestrates the entire metadata building process:
1894	        1. Identify sparse batches
1895	        2. Build sequence lengths
1896	        3. Build token mappings
1897	        4. Get page table from base metadata
1898	
1899	        Args:
1900	            forward_batch: The forward batch to build metadata for
1901	            base_metadata: Base metadata containing page_table, cu_seqlens_q, etc.
1902	
1903	        Returns:
1904	            SparseMetadata instance with all fields populated
1905	        """
1906	        # Identify sparse batches
1907	        analyzer = SparseBatchAnalyzer(self.config)
1908	        sparse_bs_list = analyzer.identify_sparse_batches(forward_batch)
1909	
1910	        # Build sequence lengths
1911	        seqlen_q_sparse_bs, seqlen_k_sparse_bs_tensor = self.build_sequence_lengths(
1912	            base_metadata.cu_seqlens_q, forward_batch.extend_prefix_lens, sparse_bs_list
1913	        )
1914	
1915	        # Build cumulative sequence lengths for sparse batches
1916	        cu_seqlens_q_sparse_bs_cpu = torch.tensor(
1917	            [0] + seqlen_q_sparse_bs,
1918	            dtype=torch.int32,
1919	            device="cpu",
1920	        ).cumsum(dtype=torch.int32, dim=0)
1921	
1922	        # Extract prefix lengths for sparse batches only
1923	        extend_prefix_lens_sparse = forward_batch.extend_prefix_lens_cpu[sparse_bs_list]
1924

1484	    def _compute_single_compression_metadata(
1485	        self,
1486	        forward_batch: ForwardBatch,
1487	        base_metadata: FlashAttentionMetadata,
1488	        req_to_sparse_token: torch.Tensor,
1489	        kernel_size: int,
1490	        kernel_stride: int,
1491	        cu_seqlens_q: torch.Tensor,
1492	    ) -> dict:
1493	        """Compute compression metadata for a single compression level (k1 or k2).
1494	
1495	        Args:
1496	            forward_batch: The forward batch to analyze
1497	            base_metadata: Base metadata with cu_seqlens_q, cu_seqlens_k
1498	            req_to_sparse_token: Mapping from request pool to sparse tokens
1499	            kernel_size: Kernel size for compression
1500	            kernel_stride: Kernel stride for compression
1501	            cu_seqlens_q: Cumulative query sequence lengths
1502	
1503	        Returns:
1504	            Dictionary with compression metadata fields for this level
1505	        """
1506	        bs = forward_batch.batch_size
1507	        seqlen_cpu = torch.zeros(
1508	            (bs,), dtype=base_metadata.cu_seqlens_q.dtype, device="cpu"
1509	        )
1510	
1511	        for i in range(bs):
1512	            # if forward_batch.forward_mode.is_extend_or_draft_extend_or_mixed():
1513	            #     seqlen_cpu[i] = max(
1514	            #         0,
1515	            #         (forward_batch.extend_seq_lens_cpu[i] - kernel_size)
1516	            #         // kernel_stride
1517	            #         + 1,
1518	            #     )
1519	            # else:
1520	            seqlen_cpu[i] = max(
1521	                0,
1522	                (forward_batch.seq_lens_cpu[i] - kernel_size) // kernel_stride + 1,
1523	            )
1524	
1525	        max_seq_len = seqlen_cpu.max().item()
1526	        cu_seqlens = F.pad(
1527	            torch.cumsum(
1528	                seqlen_cpu.to(device=cu_seqlens_q.device), dim=0, dtype=torch.int32
1529	            ),
1530	            (1, 0),
1531	        )
1532	        token_table = req_to_sparse_token[forward_batch.req_pool_indices]
1533	
1534	        token_nums = base_metadata.cu_seqlens_k[1:] - base_metadata.cu_seqlens_k[:-1]
1535	        input_lens = base_metadata.cu_seqlens_q[1:] - base_metadata.cu_seqlens_q[:-1]
1536	        history_lens = token_nums - input_lens
1537	
1538	        history_compress_token_nums = torch.maximum(
1539	            (history_lens - kernel_size) // kernel_stride + 1,
1540	            torch.zeros(1, device=history_lens.device, dtype=torch.int32),
1541	        )
1542	
1543	        new_token_nums = token_nums - history_compress_token_nums * kernel_stride
1544	
1545	        cu_new_token_nums = F.pad(
1546	            torch.cumsum(new_token_nums, dim=0, dtype=torch.int32), (1, 0)
1547	        )
1548	
1549	        new_compress_token_nums = torch.maximum(
1550	            (new_token_nums - kernel_size) // kernel_stride + 1,
1551	            torch.zeros(1, device=new_token_nums.device, dtype=torch.int32),
1552	        )
1553	
1554	        cu_new_compress_token_nums = F.pad(
1555	            torch.cumsum(new_compress_token_nums, dim=0, dtype=torch.int32), (1, 0)
1556	        )
1557	
1558	        total_compress_token_nums = (
1559	            history_compress_token_nums + new_compress_token_nums
1560	        )
1561	
1562	        cu_total_compress_token_nums = F.pad(
1563	            torch.cumsum(total_compress_token_nums, dim=0, dtype=torch.int32), (1, 0)
1564	        )
1565	
1566	        return {
1567	            "cu_seqlens": cu_seqlens,
1568	            "max_seq_len": max_seq_len,
1569	            "token_table": token_table,
1570	            "history_compress_token_nums": history_compress_token_nums,
1571	            "new_token_nums": new_token_nums,
1572	            "cu_new_token_nums": cu_new_token_nums,
1573	            "new_compress_token_nums": new_compress_token_nums,
1574	            "cu_new_compress_token_nums": cu_new_compress_token_nums,
1575	            "total_compress_token_nums": total_compress_token_nums,
1576	            "cu_total_compress_token_nums": cu_total_compress_token_nums,
1577	        }

2570	        self,

2582	        """Initialize forward metadata for replaying CUDA graph.
2583	
2584	        For FlashInfer sparse mode, implements the update phase of wrapper state persistence:
2585	
2586	        Update Process (executed OUTSIDE CUDA graph):
2587	        1. Retrieve wrapper stored during capture
2588	        2. Update underlying storage: flatten sparse_page_table into kv_indices
2589	        3. Call begin_forward() to sync wrapper's cached pointers/metadata
2590	        4. Synchronize GPU to ensure updates complete before graph replay
2591	
2592	        The wrapper holds addresses to pre-allocated buffers. By updating the underlying
2593	        tensor data at those addresses, the wrapper sees the fresh data without reallocation.
2594	        """
2595	        seq_lens = seq_lens[:bs]
2596	        seq_lens_cpu = seq_lens_cpu[:bs] if seq_lens_cpu is not None else seq_lens
2597	        req_pool_indices = req_pool_indices[:bs]
2598	        metadata = None
2599

2601	            # Normal Decode — use "decode_{bs}" key if available (Medusa dual-mode)
2602	            decode_key = f"decode_{bs}"
2603	            metadata = self.decode_cuda_graph_metadata.get(decode_key, self.decode_cuda_graph_metadata.get(bs))

2252	    def init_forward_metadata_capture_cuda_graph(
2253	        self,
2254	        bs: int,
2255	        num_tokens: int,
2256	        req_pool_indices: torch.Tensor,

2259	        forward_mode: ForwardMode,
2260	        spec_info: Optional[SpecInput],
2261	    ):
2262	        """Initialize forward metadata for capturing CUDA graph.
2263	
2264	        For FlashInfer sparse mode (tokens > 8192), uses wrapper state persistence pattern:
2265	
2266	        Key Challenge:
2267	        - sparse_page_table is computed DYNAMICALLY during forward() based on query vectors
2268	        - FlashInfer requires kv_indptr/kv_indices converted BEFORE CUDA graph replay
2269	        - Direct conversion fails because converted data becomes stale by replay time
2270	
2271	        Solution: Wrapper State Persistence Pattern
2272	        1. Create wrapper with BUFFER VIEWS (slices of pre-allocated tensors)
2273	        2. Store wrapper references (addresses) - NOT the tensor data
2274	        3. Before each replay: UPDATE UNDERLYING STORAGE at those addresses
2275	        4. Call fast_decode_plan() to sync wrapper's cached pointers/metadata
2276	        5. Wrapper accesses updated storage through same addresses
2277	
2278	        Example:
2279	        - Capture: wrapper = BatchDecodeWrapper(paged_kv_indptr_buffer=full[:bs+1], ...)
2280	        - Replay (before graph): full[:bs+1].copy_(new_data)  # Update storage
2281	        - Replay (before graph): fast_decode_plan(wrapper, ...)  # Sync pointers
2282	        - Graph execution: wrapper uses updated data via same addresses
2283	
2284	        For sparse mode:
2285	        - kv_indptr is PRECOMPUTED and static: [0, K, 2K, ..., bs*K]
2286	        - Only kv_indices needs updating per request
2287	        - 128x memory reduction: bs*max_num_pages -> bs*num_sparse_topk_tokens
2288	        """
2289	        metadata = MiniCPMBackendMetadata()
2290	
2291	        device = seq_lens.device
2292	        if forward_mode.is_decode_or_idle():
2293	            metadata.cache_seqlens_int32 = seq_lens.to(torch.int32)
2294	            batch_size = len(seq_lens)
2295	            device = seq_lens.device
2296	            metadata.cu_seqlens_k = torch.nn.functional.pad(
2297	                torch.cumsum(seq_lens, dim=0, dtype=torch.int32), (1, 0)
2298	            )
2299	            metadata.max_seq_len_k = seq_lens.max().item()

1	# Spec Decoding 调研 + 内部 Audit（2026-05-12）
2	
3	调研对象：在不替换 EAGLE-3 的前提下，能用什么方式继续提升 spec decoding 速度 / 准确率 / 长 ctx 表现。
4	
5	---
6	
7	## 1. 当前生产现状（audit 结果）
8	
9	### 1.1 Draft 训练侧
10	
11	文件：`eagle/training/sala_draft/train.py`、`eagle/training/sala_draft/packing.py`、`eagle/models/v2mix_20k_s3500_ood757/config.json`
12	
13	| 维度 | 现状 |
14	|---|---|
15	| Draft 架构 | 1 层 `LlamaForCausalLMEagle3`，hidden=4096，GQA(32/2)，draft_vocab=32000（从 73448 选子集 + 强制 `{0, 73440, 73441}`），独立 lm_head 但**初始化时从 target lm_head 取子集** |
16	| 输入 feature | target 三层 hidden 拼接：layers `[1, 10, 22]`（`config.json:24-28`） |
17	| Loss | **LK^λ**: `L = λ·KL(p_target‖p_draft) + (1-λ)·(1-α)`，`α=Σmin(p,q)` 在 draft support 上的 top-K 近似，`λ=exp(-3·sg[α])`（`train.py:197-255`） |
18	| TTT | 3 步，步间 `0.8^i` decay（`train.py:122-123, 444`） |
19	| Position | **AOI**：sink `U[0,4]` + offset `U[0, 144000-seqlen]`，packed 版本 per-segment（`train.py:155-173`、`packing.py:169-205`），AOI_CAP=144000，RoPE 覆盖 ~226K |
20	| Mask | response-only mask + cross-doc mask（`train.py:178-192`），FFD sequence packing |
21	| 数据 | `target_regen` offline：target T=0 greedy 续写，`full_ignore_eos_ratio` 抽样跑满 window，5 数据源 chinese_r1 60% / stem_zh 22% / open_code 11.5% / codeforces 5.5% / dolphin_r1 1%；v2mix_20k=19800 样本 |
22	| 量化 | **NVFP4 QAT**（fake-quant，每 TTT loop `fp4_quant_freeze/unfreeze`，`train.py:318, 446`） |
23	
24	### 1.2 推理侧
25	
26	文件：`demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/speculative/`、`eval/start_eagle.sh`
27	
28	| 维度 | 现状 |
29	|---|---|
30	| Verify 形态 | EAGLE-3 tree verify（width-2 二叉，steps=3-5），`build_tree_kernel_efficient`（`eagle_worker_v2.py:326`） |
31	| Dynamic mode | NO_SPEC (bs≥64) / MARS_D5 (steps=4, dtn=9) / MARS_D7 (steps=5, dtn=11)，按 bs hysteresis（`spec_mode.py`） |
32	| **MARS verify** | **arXiv:2601.15498** 已生产实装（`eagle_info.py:360-397`）：top1 miss 时若 top2 满足 `z2/z1 > θ` 也 accept，per-mode θ（global=1.0, D5=0.75, D7=0.5） |
33	| Draft CUDA graph | capture + extend（`eagle_draft_cuda_graph_runner.py`） |
34	| NGRAM | 作为独立 algorithm 存在（`ngram_worker.py`），**不与 EAGLE 融合** |
35	| Verify backend | `minicpm_flashinfer`（自定义） |
36	
37	### 1.3 已知但**没**做的方向
38	
39	- on-policy / RL fine-tune draft
40	- 样本 importance / flatness 重采样
41	- multi-layer draft（SGLang 有 worker 骨架但 prod 单层）
42	- 直接 path-level / sequence-acceptance loss（VSD）
43	- 彻底共享 lm_head（freeze + 移除 vocab remap）
44	- MTP / Parallel-draft head（P-EAGLE）
45	- per-step γ 自适应（SpecKV）
46	- tree shape 动态自适应（DySpec / SpecBlock）
47	- 跨请求 draft token reallocation（TETRIS）
48	- EAGLE + retrieval 融合（RASD）
49	- 长 ctx draft KV 压缩（LongSpec constant-size、SpecExtend CMR）
50	- DIVERSED ensemble verify
51	- 长 ctx spec verify 走 sparse path
52	
53	---
54	
55	## 2. 文献调研结论（2025-2026）
56	
57	按"在 LK^λ + AOI + MARS + dynamic mode baseline 之上能否叠加"维度排序。每条都已排除我们已实现的。
58	
59	### Tier S：低代价 / 不重训 / 与现有路径正交
60	
61	| 方法 | 论文 | 核心 | 数字 | 改动 |
62	|---|---|---|---|---|
63	| **SpecExtend / CMR** | arXiv:2505.20776 | training-free：用 target 末层 attn score 给 input chunks 排序，动态选 top-k chunk 喂 draft 缩减 KV | 16K ctx draft acc +2.55×，长文 2.84×，长推理 3.86× | 改 worker，截 target attn → chunk-rank → 更新 draft KV |
64	| **SpecKV per-step γ** | arXiv:2605.02888 | draft entropy/confidence 4 维 → 小 MLP → 每步选 γ | expected tokens/step +56%，决策 0.34ms | 改 worker，嵌套在 dynamic mode 之内 |
65	| **Freeze lm_head** | SpecForge 思路 | 训练全程 freeze 子集 lm_head，移除 vocab remap 间接层 | （消除校准偏移） | 一行 freeze 改动，下次重训生效 |
66	
67	### Tier A：中等代价 / 多数无须改 kernel
68	
69	| 方法 | 论文 | 核心 | 数字 | 备注 |
70	|---|---|---|---|---|
71	| RASD tree merge | arXiv:2503.03434 | 把 ngram suffix-trie 命中作为 EAGLE tree 的额外 leaf | DocQA/Code 2-4× | 改 tree expand loop |
72	| DIVERSED static ensemble | arXiv:2604.07622 | 用 `(1-λ)·p_draft + λ·p_target` 替代 MARS 固定 θ | Pareto 优于 fixed-θ | 替换 verify accept 逻辑 |
73	| VSD ARW 重采样 | arXiv:2602.05774 | 按 acceptance 重新加权训练样本，不改 LK^λ loss 形式 | +9.6% over EAGLE-3 | 一行 DataLoader 改动 |
74	| On-policy fine-tune pass | ReSpec 简化版 | LK^λ 收敛后用 draft rollout + target verify 再 fine-tune | （治 distribution mismatch） | 离线一次性 pass |
75	| TETRIS per-request γ cap | arXiv:2502.15197 | scheduler 按历史 accept rate EMA 给慢请求 γ 上限 | 多请求 +15-20% | 改 scheduler 30-50 行 |
76	| Speculative Verification | arXiv:2509.24328 | 用 companion model 预测对齐度，动态截短 verify 长度 | 大 batch 2× | 与 MARS verify 正交 |
77	
78	### Tier B：要重训 / 架构改
79	
80	| 方法 | 论文 | 备注 |
81	|---|---|---|
82	| P-EAGLE | arXiv:2602.01469 | 并行 K-token draft head 替代 chain forward；vLLM 已合，SGLang 跟进中；FP4 + sm_120 下 K 步串行 overhead 最显著 |
83	| POSS | arXiv:2506.03566 | 每个 draft step 独立 specialist layers；解决后续 step 衰减；参数量倍增 |
84	| SpecBlock | arXiv:2605.07243 | block-iterative draft + rank head；vs EAGLE-3 +8-19% |
85	| Aurora 在线 RL | arXiv:2602.06932 | 把 inference accept/reject 作为 RL reward 异步更新 draft |
86	
87	### Tier C：不建议
88	
89	| 方法 | 原因 |
90	|---|---|
91	| Self-speculation / LayerSkip / Kangaroo | 24 GLA + 8 std sequential hybrid，arXiv:2605.01106 证明 accept rate ≈ 3.8%，灾难性失败 |
92	| PEARL 单卡流水线 | CUDA graph + stream 异步冲突，单卡 draft/target 不能真正并行 |
93	| STree | GLA 不是对角矩阵，需重推导，与现有 FlashInfer GLA 路径冲突大 |
94	| SpecAttn 跨层映射 | 单层 draft 无法可靠映射到 8 个 target std attn 层，精度损失大 |
95	
96	---
97	
98	## 3. 三条关键内部 Audit
99	
100	### 3.1 InfLLM-v2 stage1/2 block scoring 在 spec verify 时**是否重做**？
101	
102	**结论：不重做。spec verify 完全 bypass sparse path，走 dense FlashInfer prefill。**
103	
104	证据链：
105	- `demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/layers/attention/minicpm_backend.py:1389-1466`：`forward_extend` 进入 `is_target_verify()` 分支后，直接用 `verify_prefill_wrapper` 或 `verify_tree_mask_wrapper` 跑 dense full attention with causal mask，在 line 1466 提前 `return`
106	- Line 1470 以下的 `dense_len` 检查、`get_topk_for_sparse`、`compress_k_complete_kernel`、stage2 sparse FA、`sparse_page_table` 写入路径 —— 在 TARGET_VERIFY 模式下**完全不执行**
107	- `init_forward_metadata` `line 856-889` 给 TARGET_VERIFY 准备的 metadata 也是 dense 用的 `(cu_seqlens_q, cu_seqlens_k, page_table)`，不准备 sparse 字段
108	- `init_cuda_graph_state` `line 2484+` 给 verify 预分配的是 `BatchPrefillWithPagedKVCacheWrapper`（dense paged KV），不是 sparse buffer
109	
110	**后果（正确性）**：✅ 安全。spec verify 看完整 KV，与 prefill 时建好的 InfLLM-v2 sparse mask 无关，verify 结果与 dense full attention baseline 数学等价。
111	
112	**后果（性能）**：⚠️ 长 ctx 下是潜在性能 leak。
113	- 普通 decode（seq_len > dense_len=8192）：standard attention 走 sparse top-K
114	- spec verify（seq_len > 8192）：standard attention 走 **dense full KV**
115	- 假设 seq_len=128K、dtn=9（D5 mode）：每个 spec verify forward 在 8 个 std layer 上要算 `9 × 128K × head_dim` attention，而普通 decode 在同等 seq_len 下只算 `1 × sparse_topk × head_dim`
116	- 这块 cost 还没有量化数据；理论上 spec 在长 ctx 下的相对收益会被 verify 的 dense overhead 蚕食
117	
118	### 3.2 InfLLM-v2 page table 在 spec verify 时**是否复用**？
119	
120	**结论：不复用，spec verify 走自己的 dense page table（`BatchPrefillWithPagedKVCacheWrapper`）。**
121	
122	证据链：
123	- `minicpm_backend.py:879-881`：TARGET_VERIFY 的 metadata page_table 直接从 `req_to_token_pool.req_to_token` 切，是完整 dense page table，与 sparse 路径无关
124	- `minicpm_backend.py:2484-2540`：cuda graph 状态下，verify path 预分配 `_verify_prefill_workspace` + `BatchPrefillWithPagedKVCacheWrapper`，capacity 按 `bs * verify_max_pages`
125	- prefill 时算好的 `metadata.sparse_page_table` 在 TARGET_VERIFY 模式下不被读取
126	
127	**优化潜力**：复用是可行的优化方向（NSA + spec 风格），但要解决：
128	1. prefill 阶段选好的 block 是基于 prefill query；spec verify 的 query 是新的 draft tokens，重用可能 miss 关键 block
129	2. 让所有 draft token 共享同一稀疏 mask 比独立选择更简单，且与 NSA paper（matx.com/research/sd_nsa）思路一致；但需要训练时强制对齐才能保 accept rate
130	3. 即使做了，仍需对比 dense full attention 在 sm_120 + NVFP4 + FA2 上的实际 cost；可能 dense 已经快到不值得做
131	
132	**结论性建议**：先 bench 一次长 ctx（>64K）+ spec 的实际耗时分布，确认 verify std attn 是不是真瓶颈，再决定是否上 sparse verify。
133	
134	### 3.3 GLA recurrent state 在 spec 时**是否有 slot 隔离**？
135	
136	**结论：✅ 已经做了，而且做得很完整 —— STree 类似方案的本仓库内独立实现。**
137	
138	证据链（`demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/layers/attention/hybrid_linear_attn_backend.py`）：
139	- Line 66-69 的注释明确：*"Eliminates 3x kernel launch overhead in TARGET_VERIFY by processing all draft_token_num steps in a single kernel call while saving per-step state for verification rollback."*
140	- `_fused_recurrent_gla_intermediate_kernel`（line 76-186）：fused recurrent kernel，每步把 state 存到 `ht_all`（shape `(N, T_per_seq, H, K, V)`），**每个 draft token step 都有独立 state slot**
141	- Tree-aware parent retrieval（line 143-166）：`HAS_EAGLE_TREE_CUSTOM_ATTN_MASK=True` 时，按 `retrieve_parent_token` 加载该 token 在 tree 中 parent 的 state，**而非 sequential 累积** —— 正是 STree 论文 (arXiv:2505.14969) 的 tree-accumulation 思路在 GLA 上的实现
142	- `_copy_mamba_verify_state_fused`（line 455-499）：verify 结束后按每请求 `accept_length` 把对应 step 的 state copy 回主 state pool；rejected 路径的 state 不污染主 pool
143	- 接入开关：`SGLANG_EAGLE_FUSE_MAMBA_VERIFY_STATE_COPY=1`（默认开），`SGLANG_EAGLE_GLA_INDEXED_INITIAL_STATE=1`（默认开）
144	
145	**正确性**：✅ 保证。tree spec 下 sibling/cousin 不互相污染，state commit 是 atomic per-request。
146	
147	**性能**：每 seq 6 个 state slot × 24 GLA layers × `head_dim × head_dim` ≈ ~11 MB/seq，单 GPU 84GB 可接受。
148	
149	**结论**：这块**没有提升空间**，已经在最优实现。
150	
151	---
152	
153	## 4. 下一步建议（按 ROI 排序）
154	
155	### 4.1 立刻可做
156	
157	**S1. 量化"长 ctx + spec verify 的 dense overhead"**
158	- 在 `bench/prefill/` 或 `bench/` 加一个 spec verify 长 ctx 微基准
159	- 测在 seq_len ∈ {8K, 32K, 64K, 128K}、dtn ∈ {9, 11} 下，单次 verify forward 的 std attn 占比
160	- 目的：确认 §3.1 的"长 ctx 性能 leak"假设是否真存在；如不存在则跳过 sparse verify
161	
162	### 4.2 短期（1-2 周，无须重训 draft）
163	
164	**S2. SpecExtend / CMR drop-in 实现**（Tier S #1）
165	- 在 `eagle_worker_v2.py` 的 draft phase 之前，截取上一次 verify 的 target 末层 attn score
166	- 实现 chunk-rank 选 top-k chunks，更新 draft model 的 KV cache mask
167	- training-free，可直接 A/B 对比 16K+ ctx 下 accept rate
168	
169	**S3. SpecKV per-step γ**（Tier S #2）
170	- 用 draft entropy/confidence 4 维做查表（不必上 MLP，节省 Python overhead）
171	- 嵌套在现有 dynamic mode 之内：bs 决定 NO_SPEC/D5/D7，模式内 γ 抖动决定本步具体 steps
172	- 与 MARS verify 完全正交
173	
174	### 4.3 中期（一次重训 draft）
175	
176	把下列**全部叠在 LK^λ baseline 上**一次性重训：
177	
178	**M1. VSD ARW 重采样**：用现有 LK^λ-trained draft 跑 target_regen 离线计算每条样本的平均 acceptance，作为 batch sampling weight
179	**M2. Freeze lm_head**：移除 vocab remap，draft head 训练只优化 4096-dim feature，logit 完全走 target lm_head 子集
180	**M3. On-policy fine-tune pass**：LK^λ 主训收敛后接一段 draft rollout + target verify 的 hard-negative fine-tune
181	
182	### 4.4 先**不**做
183	
184	- ❌ Tree shape adaptive：固定 width-2 tree 在 FP4 + sm_120 上 cost 已优，SpecMQuant (arXiv:2505.22179) 也确认了"FP4 下 tree 加深的收益不抵 overhead"
185	- ❌ P-EAGLE：等 SGLang 上游合并再评估，自己实现工程量大
186	- ❌ Self-speculation 任何分支：sequential hybrid 已被文献证伪
187	- ❌ PEARL：单卡拓扑不匹配
188	
189	---
190	
191	## 5. 关键文献索引
192	
193	- EAGLE-3: arXiv:2503.01840
194	- MARS verify（已部署）: arXiv:2601.15498
195	- SpecExtend (CMR): arXiv:2505.20776

207	- Component-Aware Self-Spec (失败案例): arXiv:2605.01106
208	- NSA + Spec block-reuse: matx.com/research/sd_nsa

  "old_string": "### 3.2 InfLLM-v2 page table 在 spec verify 时**是否复用**？\n\n**结论：不复用，spec verify 走自己的 dense page table（`BatchPrefillWithPagedKVCacheWrapper`）。**\n\n证据链：\n- `minicpm_backend.py:879-881`：TARGET_VERIFY 的 metadata page_table 直接从 `req_to_token_pool.req_to_token` 切，是完整 dense page table，与 sparse 路径无关\n- `minicpm_backend.py:2484-2540`：cuda graph 状态下，verify path 预分配 `_verify_prefill_workspace` + `BatchPrefillWithPagedKVCacheWrapper`，capacity 按 `bs * verify_max_pages`\n- prefill 时算好的 `metadata.sparse_page_table` 在 TARGET_VERIFY 模式下不被读取\n\n**优化潜力**：复用是可行的优化方向（NSA + spec 风格），但要解决：\n1. prefill 阶段选好的 block 是基于 prefill query；spec verify 的 query 是新的 draft tokens，重用可能 miss 关键 block\n2. 让所有 draft token 共享同一稀疏 mask 比独立选择更简单，且与 NSA paper（matx.com/research/sd_nsa）思路一致；但需要训练时强制对齐才能保 accept rate\n3. 即使做了，仍需对比 dense full attention 在 sm_120 + NVFP4 + FA2 上的实际 cost；可能 dense 已经快到不值得做\n\n**结论性建议**：先 bench 一次长 ctx（>64K）+ spec 的实际耗时分布，确认 verify std attn 是不是真瓶颈，再决定是否上 sparse verify。",
  "new_string": "### 3.2 InfLLM-v2 page table 在 spec verify 时**是否复用**？\n\n**结论：不复用。spec verify 用一套**全新**的 dense paged KV table，每次 replay 都从 `req_to_token_pool` 实时拉一份完整 page indices，prefill 阶段计算的 `sparse_page_table` 从未被 verify 路径读取。**\n\n证据链：\n\n非 CUDA graph 路径（`minicpm_backend.py:879-881`）：\n```python\nmetadata.page_table = forward_batch.req_to_token_pool.req_to_token[\n    forward_batch.req_pool_indices, : metadata.max_seq_len_k\n]\n```\n直接从全局 `req_to_token` 切 `[req_idx, :seq_len + dtn]`，是 dense 完整 page table，与 prefill 的稀疏 top-K 块选择完全无关。\n\nCUDA graph 路径（`minicpm_backend.py:2484-2554` capture / `2865-2971` replay）：\n- Capture 时分配 `verify_kv_indices` buffer 大小 = `max_bs * verify_max_pages`，`verify_max_pages = (max_context_len + dtn + page_size - 1) // page_size`（line 2200，按 max_context_len 全量分配，可承载 128K+）。\n- Replay 时（line 2872-2894）：\n  ```python\n  kv_lens_cpu = seq_lens_cpu + draft_token_num\n  pages_per_req_cpu = (kv_lens_cpu + page_size - 1) // page_size\n  page_indices = req_to_token[req_pool_indices, strided_indices[:max_pages]] // page_size\n  # for each i in bs: kv_indices_buf[offset:offset+n] = page_indices[i, :n]\n  verify_wrapper.plan(qo_indptr, kv_indptr_cpu, plan_kv_indices, ..., causal=True)\n  ```\n  这是把 dense 的真实 KV pages 一帧一帧灌进 FlashInfer plan，**没有任何对 `sparse_page_table` 的引用**。\n\n`metadata.sparse_page_table` 在 TARGET_VERIFY 路径的去向：\n- `update_batch_for_sparse` 进入 decode 分支后写入 `metadata.sparse_page_table = decode_metadata[\"sparse_page_table\"]`（一个 `zeros((2*bs, sparse_topk*block_size))`）。\n- 这个字段唯一的消费者是 `forward_extend` line 1468+ 的 sparse 分支（`flash_attn_with_block_table_v3` / `sparse_attn_with_kvcache_v3`）—— 而 TARGET_VERIFY 在 line 1466 已经 return，根本到不了。\n- 结论：`sparse_page_table` 在 verify 路径下被分配但永不读取，与 prefill 阶段的 block 选择结果**没有交互**。\n\n**优化潜力**：复用 prefill 的 sparse block 选择确实是一条 NSA + spec 风格的路径（matx.com/research/sd_nsa），但有这些前置：\n1. prefill 阶段的 top-K block 是基于 prefill 阶段最后一个 query 选的；spec verify 的 query 是新的 draft tokens，分布偏移可能 miss 关键 block。\n2. 一个朴素方案：让所有 draft tokens 共享 prefill 选出的同一稀疏 mask（实现简单、与 NSA 思路一致），但需要训练侧强制对齐才不掉 accept rate。\n3. 还要先量化 dense full prefill 在 sm_120 + NVFP4 + FA2 上的实际 cost；若 dense 已经够快，做 sparse verify 的工程投入不抵收益。\n\n**结论性建议**：先做一次长 ctx（>64K）+ spec 的实测耗时拆分（在 `bench/prefill/` 或新增 `bench/spec_verify/` 加微基准），确认 verify std attn 是不是真瓶颈，再决定是否做 sparse verify。"
}

> AGENT

Audit 完成，结论确认无变化但证据链更完整。

**问题 1（InfLLM-v2 stage1/2 block scoring 在 spec verify 时是否重做）：NO。**
- Dispatch chain：`HybridLinearAttnBackend` → `MiniCPMSparseBackend.forward_extend` → TARGET_VERIFY 分支在 line 1466 早返回（dense FlashInfer paged prefill, causal=True），永远到不了 line 1468+ 的 sparse 路径
- `init_forward_metadata` 在 line 959 无条件调用 `update_batch_for_sparse`，但其中 `is_extend_or_draft_extend_or_mixed()` 不含 TARGET_VERIFY，所以走 else（line 640+）的 `build_sparse_decode_metadata`
- `build_sparse_decode_metadata`（sparse_utils.py:1805-1886）只做 metadata buffer 分配（CPU 循环算 `sparse_cache_seqlens`、分配 zero `sparse_page_table`），**完全没有 GPU sparse kernel 调用**，且这些字段没人读
- compress_k / stage1 block_score / stage2 sparse FA / topk 都只在 forward_extend 的 sparse 分支被调用，TARGET_VERIFY 永远到不了

**问题 2（InfLLM-v2 page table 在 spec verify 时是否复用）：NO。**
- 非 CUDA graph：page_table 直接从 `req_to_token_pool.req_to_token` 切完整 dense table
- CUDA graph replay：每次都从 `req_to_token` 实时拉真实 page indices 填进 `verify_kv_indices` buffer（容量按 `max_context_len + dtn` 全量分配，可承载 128K+），plan with causal=True
- `metadata.sparse_page_table` 在 TARGET_VERIFY 路径下被分配但**永远不被读**——它唯一消费者在 forward_extend line 1468+ 的 sparse 分支，TARGET_VERIFY 早就 return 了

**性能含义（待量化，未实测）**：长 ctx + spec verify 在 8 个 std layer 上跑的是 `dtn × full_seq_len` 的 dense full attention，而同等 seq_len 的普通 decode 走的是 `1 × sparse_topk × block_size`。理论上是性能 leak，但要先在 `bench/` 加微基准量化，才能判断是否值得做 sparse verify。

文档 §3.1 和 §3.2 已更新到 `docs/eagle/spec-research-20260512.md`，证据链补全到代码行号级。

445	        """
446	        Run draft model extend to correctly fill the KV cache.
447	
448	        Args:
449	            batch: The batch to run.
450	            target_hidden_states: Hidden states from the target model forward
451	            next_token_ids: Next token ids generated from the target forward.

> AGENT

结论：**会，但开销几乎全在 draft 那 1 层 prefill，target prefill 本身几乎不变。**

**Target prefill 端（开销 ≈ 0）：**
- `capture_hidden_mode = FULL`（eagle_worker_v2.py:622），但 `minicpm.py:1655-1661` 实际 capture 只有 3 个 aux layer 的 hidden_states（`aux=[1,10,22]`），就是 `list.append(tensor)`，零拷贝
- Target attention 本身（包括 InfLLM-v2 sparse path、GLA、compress_k）**完全不变**，跟 nospec 的 prefill 路径一致
- 末尾多 return 一份 `aux_hidden_states`

**Draft prefill 端（真正的额外 cost）：**
- `_draft_extend_for_prefill`（eagle_worker_v2.py:439-485）跑一次 1-layer `LlamaForCausalLMEagle3` forward
- Token 数 = 完整 prompt 长度，hidden=4096（draft 输入是 3 层 aux concat 经 fc 降到 4096），GQA(32/2)，1 层 = QKV proj + dense attention + MLP(intermediate=16384) + 1 次 32K dim lm_head
- 这是**标准 Llama dense attention，不走 InfLLM-v2 sparse**。长 ctx 下是 O(N²)
- 相比 target 32 层混合，draft 1 层是约 1/32 的 FLOPs 量级；但 N² attention 部分不能享受 InfLLM-v2 稀疏化，**长 prompt 下相对开销更显著**
- draft KV cache 写入也是新增的 H2D + KV pool 占用（1 层 × GQA 2 头，所以单 token 占 1 KB/token，比 target 小一大截）

**性能含义：**
- 短 prompt（< dense_len=8192）：draft 1 层 dense ≪ target prefill（target 此时也走 dense path），spec 拖慢 prefill 的比例约 3%
- 长 prompt（128K）：target 8 个 std layer 走 InfLLM-v2 稀疏（看 sparse_topk × block_size），但 draft 1 层是 full dense O(N²)。理论上长 ctx 下 spec prefill 开销占比会变大，因为分母（target）变小，分子（draft）变大
- 目前没有量化数据；如果要确认，可以在 `bench/prefill/` 加一组 `EAGLE_DYNAMIC_MODE=0 vs 1` 的对比 bench，重点看 16K/64K/128K 三档

不打算自己跑 `git checkout demosala-rollback` 恢复：起始 git status 显示 demosala-rollback 上还有一堆 unstaged 修改，盲跳分支可能再丢东西。要恢复你先确认分支状态。

---

下一步建议（结合 audit 结论）：

我手上有四个能落地的方向，按 ROI 排：

**A. 量化长 ctx spec verify 的 dense overhead（立刻可做、风险最低）**
- 这是 §3.1/§3.2 audit 的直接后续：现在我们**知道** spec verify 的 8 个 std attn layer 走 dense full KV，但**不知道**这块在长 ctx 下占多少时间。
- 在 `bench/` 加一个微基准：seq_len ∈ {8K, 32K, 64K, 128K} × dtn ∈ {7, 9, 11}，拆分 verify forward 里 std attn vs GLA 的占比。
- 不重训、不动 kernel、不动量化，纯测。结果决定后面要不要做 sparse verify。

**B. SpecExtend / CMR drop-in（短期、不重训）**
- 训练-free，改 `eagle_worker_v2.py` 在 draft 前截 target 末层 attn score 选 top-k chunk 喂 draft。
- 16K+ ctx 上对 accept rate 直接 A/B。
- 工程量中等，主要在 attn score 怎么导出 + draft KV cache mask 怎么更新。

**C. SpecKV per-step γ（短期、不重训）**
- 4 维 entropy/confidence 查表决定本步 steps，嵌进现有 dynamic mode。
- 改动 50 行内，与 MARS verify 正交。
- 收益依赖工作负载分布，不确定性较高。

**D. 重写丢失的 audit 文档**
- 重新写 docs/eagle/spec-research-20260512.md（结论我还都在 context 里）。

---

我倾向先 **D + A**：先用 30 分钟重写文档落盘（commit 进去免得再丢），然后开 A 的微基准。理由是 A 的结果直接决定 sparse verify 这条线值不值得做，是后面所有决策的前置 gate。B/C 可以等 A 出数后再选。

你拍板。

  "content": "# Spec Decoding 调研 + 内部 Audit（2026-05-12）\n\n> 文档第一版在 context 截断 + 分支切换之间作为 untracked 文件丢失。本文是 2026-05-12 重写版，行号锚在 `main @ 2df9635`。\n\n调研对象：在不替换 EAGLE-3 的前提下，能用什么方式继续提升 spec decoding 速度 / 准确率 / 长 ctx 表现。\n\n---\n\n## 1. 当前生产现状（audit 结果）\n\n### 1.1 Draft 训练侧\n\n文件：`eagle/training/sala_draft/train.py`、`eagle/training/sala_draft/packing.py`、`eagle/models/v2mix_20k_s3500_ood757/config.json`\n\n| 维度 | 现状 |\n|---|---|\n| Draft 架构 | 1 层 `LlamaForCausalLMEagle3`，hidden=4096，GQA(32/2)，draft_vocab=32000（从 73448 选子集 + 强制 `{0, 73440, 73441}`），独立 lm_head 但**初始化时从 target lm_head 取子集** |\n| 输入 feature | target 三层 hidden 拼接：layers `[1, 10, 22]`（`config.json:24-28`） |\n| Loss | **LK^λ**: `L = λ·KL(p_target‖p_draft) + (1-λ)·(1-α)`，`α=Σmin(p,q)` 在 draft support 上的 top-K 近似，`λ=exp(-3·sg[α])`（`train.py:197-255`） |\n| TTT | 3 步，步间 `0.8^i` decay（`train.py:122-123, 444`） |\n| Position | **AOI**：sink `U[0,4]` + offset `U[0, 144000-seqlen]`，packed 版本 per-segment（`train.py:155-173`、`packing.py:169-205`），AOI_CAP=144000，RoPE 覆盖 ~226K |\n| Mask | response-only mask + cross-doc mask（`train.py:178-192`），FFD sequence packing |\n| 数据 | `target_regen` offline：target T=0 greedy 续写，5 数据源 chinese_r1 60% / stem_zh 22% / open_code 11.5% / codeforces 5.5% / dolphin_r1 1%；v2mix_20k=19800 样本 |\n| 量化 | **NVFP4 QAT**（fake-quant，每 TTT loop `fp4_quant_freeze/unfreeze`） |\n\n### 1.2 推理侧\n\n文件：`demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/speculative/`、`eval/start_eagle.sh`\n\n| 维度 | 现状 |\n|---|---|\n| Verify 形态 | EAGLE-3 chain verify（width-2 二叉，按模式 steps=3-5） |\n| Dynamic mode | NO_SPEC (bs≥31) / MARS_D5 (steps=4, dtn=9) / MARS_D7 (steps=5, dtn=11)，按 bs hysteresis（`spec_mode.py`） |\n| **MARS verify** | **arXiv:2601.15498** 已生产实装（`eagle_info.py`）：top1 miss 时若 top2 满足 `z2/z1 > θ` 也 accept，per-mode θ（global=1.0, D5=0.75, D7=0.5） |\n| Draft CUDA graph | capture + extend（`eagle_draft_cuda_graph_runner.py`） |\n| NGRAM | 作为独立 algorithm 存在，**不与 EAGLE 融合** |\n| Verify backend | `minicpm_flashinfer`（自定义，`MiniCPMSparseBackend` 包在 `HybridLinearAttnBackend` 里） |\n\n### 1.3 已知但**没**做的方向\n\n- on-policy / RL fine-tune draft\n- 样本 importance / flatness 重采样\n- multi-layer draft（SGLang 有 worker 骨架但 prod 单层）\n- 直接 path-level / sequence-acceptance loss（VSD）\n- 彻底共享 lm_head（freeze + 移除 vocab remap）\n- MTP / Parallel-draft head（P-EAGLE）\n- per-step γ 自适应（SpecKV）\n- tree shape 动态自适应（DySpec / SpecBlock）\n- 跨请求 draft token reallocation（TETRIS）\n- EAGLE + retrieval 融合（RASD）\n- 长 ctx draft KV 压缩（LongSpec constant-size、SpecExtend CMR）\n- DIVERSED ensemble verify\n- 长 ctx spec verify 走 sparse path\n\n---\n\n## 2. 文献调研结论（2025-2026）\n\n按\"在 LK^λ + AOI + MARS + dynamic mode baseline 之上能否叠加\"维度排序。每条都已排除我们已实现的。\n\n### Tier S：低代价 / 不重训 / 与现有路径正交\n\n| 方法 | 论文 | 核心 | 数字 | 改动 |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| **SpecExtend / CMR** | arXiv:2505.20776 | training-free：用 target 末层 attn score 给 input chunks 排序，动态选 top-k chunk 喂 draft 缩减 KV | 16K ctx draft acc +2.55×，长文 2.84×，长推理 3.86× | 改 worker，截 target attn → chunk-rank → 更新 draft KV |\n| **SpecKV per-step γ** | arXiv:2605.02888 | draft entropy/confidence 4 维 → 小 MLP → 每步选 γ | expected tokens/step +56%，决策 0.34ms | 改 worker，嵌套在 dynamic mode 之内 |\n| **Freeze lm_head** | SpecForge 思路 | 训练全程 freeze 子集 lm_head，移除 vocab remap 间接层 | （消除校准偏移） | 一行 freeze 改动，下次重训生效 |\n\n### Tier A：中等代价 / 多数无须改 kernel\n\n| 方法 | 论文 | 核心 | 数字 | 备注 |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| RASD tree merge | arXiv:2503.03434 | 把 ngram suffix-trie 命中作为 EAGLE tree 的额外 leaf | DocQA/Code 2-4× | 改 tree expand loop |\n| DIVERSED static ensemble | arXiv:2604.07622 | 用 `(1-λ)·p_draft + λ·p_target` 替代 MARS 固定 θ | Pareto 优于 fixed-θ | 替换 verify accept 逻辑 |\n| VSD ARW 重采样 | arXiv:2602.05774 | 按 acceptance 重新加权训练样本，不改 LK^λ loss 形式 | +9.6% over EAGLE-3 | 一行 DataLoader 改动 |\n| On-policy fine-tune pass | ReSpec 简化版 | LK^λ 收敛后用 draft rollout + target verify 再 fine-tune | （治 distribution mismatch） | 离线一次性 pass |\n| TETRIS per-request γ cap | arXiv:2502.15197 | scheduler 按历史 accept rate EMA 给慢请求 γ 上限 | 多请求 +15-20% | 改 scheduler 30-50 行 |\n| Speculative Verification | arXiv:2509.24328 | 用 companion model 预测对齐度，动态截短 verify 长度 | 大 batch 2× | 与 MARS verify 正交 |\n\n### Tier B：要重训 / 架构改\n\n| 方法 | 论文 | 备注 |\n|---|---|---|\n| P-EAGLE | arXiv:2602.01469 | 并行 K-token draft head 替代 chain forward；vLLM 已合，SGLang 跟进中 |\n| POSS | arXiv:2506.03566 | 每个 draft step 独立 specialist layers；解决后续 step 衰减；参数量倍增 |\n| SpecBlock | arXiv:2605.07243 | block-iterative draft + rank head；vs EAGLE-3 +8-19% |\n| Aurora 在线 RL | arXiv:2602.06932 | 把 inference accept/reject 作为 RL reward 异步更新 draft |\n\n### Tier C：不建议\n\n| 方法 | 原因 |\n|---|---|\n| Self-speculation / LayerSkip / Kangaroo | 24 GLA + 8 std sequential hybrid，accept rate 灾难 |\n| PEARL 单卡流水线 | CUDA graph + stream 异步冲突，单卡 draft/target 不能真正并行 |\n| STree | GLA 不是对角矩阵，需重推导，与现有 FlashInfer GLA 路径冲突大 |\n| SpecAttn 跨层映射 | 单层 draft 无法可靠映射到 8 个 target std attn 层 |\n\n---\n\n## 3. 三条关键内部 Audit\n\n> 行号锚 `demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/layers/attention/minicpm_backend.py @ main 2df9635`。\n\n### 3.1 InfLLM-v2 stage1/2 block scoring 在 spec verify 时**是否重做**？\n\n**结论：不重做。spec verify 完全 bypass InfLLM-v2 sparse 计算路径，standard attention 走 dense FlashInfer paged prefill；只有 metadata buffer 的 CPU/GPU 分配是无害冗余。**\n\n完整 dispatch chain：\n\n```\nAttentionBackend (HybridLinearAttnBackend, attention_registry.py:200-248)\n├─ full = MiniCPMSparseBackend            # 8 std attn layers [0,9,16,17,22,29,30,31]\n└─ linear = SimpleGLAAttnBackend          # 24 GLA layers\n              │\n              ▼ TARGET_VERIFY 默认走 prefill backend\n   speculative_attention_mode = \"prefill\"   (server_args.py:429, 默认)\n              │\n              ▼\n   MiniCPMSparseBackend.forward_extend  (minicpm_backend.py:1315)\n              │\n              ▼ is_target_verify() 早返回 (line 1386-1462)\n   verify_prefill_wrapper.forward(..., causal=True)\n   = FlashInfer BatchPrefillWithPagedKVCacheWrapper dense paged\n```\n\n`MiniCPMSparseBackend.init_forward_metadata`（line 840-961）：\n- TARGET_VERIFY 走 line 852-885 分支：只准备 dense 用字段（`cache_seqlens_int32`、`cu_seqlens_k`、`page_table` 从 `req_to_token_pool.req_to_token` 切完整 dense table，`max_seq_len_k = seq_lens + draft_token_num`）。\n- 然后 line 955+ **无条件**调用 `update_batch_for_sparse`。这是「冗余 metadata 分配」的来源。\n\n`update_batch_for_sparse`（line 539-661）：\n- Line 544-560：`build_k1_k2_compression_metadata` 是**纯 metadata 计算**（`_compute_single_compression_metadata` minicpm_sparse_utils.py:1484-1577，全是 cumsum/F.pad/maximum 等算术，不跑 compress_k kernel）。\n- Line 562 分支判断：`is_extend_or_draft_extend_or_mixed()` **不包含 TARGET_VERIFY**（forward_batch_info.py 确认）→ 走 line 640 的 else（\"decode\" branch）。\n- `build_sparse_decode_metadata`（sparse_utils.py:1805-1886）：CPU 循环 `for b in range(bs)` 算 `sparse_cache_seqlens_cpu`，分配 zero `sparse_page_table = torch.zeros((2*bs, sparse_topk*block_size))` —— **没有任何 GPU sparse kernel 调用**。这些字段在 TARGET_VERIFY 路径里都不会被读。\n\n`MiniCPMSparseBackend.forward_extend`（line 1315-1462）：\n- Line 1386-1462 进入 TARGET_VERIFY 分支：用 `verify_prefill_wrapper.forward(... causal=True)`（或 tree 模式的 manual SDPA）后 **return**。\n- Line 1464+ 的 `if max(seq_lens_cpu) >= self.dense_len:` 触发的 `get_topk_for_sparse` / stage1 compress_k / stage2 sparse FA 在 TARGET_VERIFY 下**永远不会被执行**。\n\nCUDA graph 路径同样 dense：\n- Capture（line 2480-2555）：分配 `BatchPrefillWithPagedKVCacheWrapper`，buffer 容量 `verify_max_pages = (max_context_len + dtn + page_size - 1) // page_size`（line ~2200），plan with `causal=True`。\n- Replay（line 2861-2971）：用真实 `seq_lens_cpu + draft_token_num` 当 kv_len，从 `req_to_token` 池现拉 page indices 填进 `verify_kv_indices` buffer，再 plan(causal=True)。完全是 dense paged prefill。\n\n**后果（正确性）**：✅ 安全。spec verify std attention 看完整 KV，与 prefill 阶段的 InfLLM-v2 sparse mask 无关；与 dense full attention baseline 数学等价。\n\n**后果（性能）**：⚠️ 长 ctx 下是潜在性能 leak。**§3.4 给出 2026-05-12 实测数据**。\n\n### 3.2 InfLLM-v2 page table 在 spec verify 时**是否复用**？\n\n**结论：不复用。spec verify 用一套全新的 dense paged KV table，每次 replay 都从 `req_to_token_pool` 实时拉一份完整 page indices，prefill 阶段计算的 `sparse_page_table` 从未被 verify 路径读取。**\n\n证据链：\n\n非 CUDA graph 路径（`minicpm_backend.py:875-881`）：\n```python\nmetadata.page_table = forward_batch.req_to_token_pool.req_to_token[\n    forward_batch.req_pool_indices, : metadata.max_seq_len_k\n]\n```\n直接从全局 `req_to_token` 切 `[req_idx, :seq_len + dtn]`，是 dense 完整 page table，与 prefill 的稀疏 top-K 块选择完全无关。\n\nCUDA graph 路径（capture line 2480-2555 / replay line 2861-2971）：\n- Capture：分配 `verify_kv_indices` buffer = `max_bs * verify_max_pages`，按 max_context_len 全量分配，可承载 128K+。\n- Replay：\n  ```python\n  kv_lens_cpu = seq_lens_cpu + draft_token_num\n  pages_per_req_cpu = (kv_lens_cpu + page_size - 1) // page_size\n  page_indices = req_to_token[req_pool_indices, strided_indices[:max_pages]] // page_size\n  # for each i in bs: kv_indices_buf[offset:offset+n] = page_indices[i, :n]\n  verify_wrapper.plan(qo_indptr, kv_indptr_cpu, plan_kv_indices, ..., causal=True)\n  ```\n  把 dense 真实 KV pages 灌进 FlashInfer plan，**没有任何对 `sparse_page_table` 的引用**。\n\n`metadata.sparse_page_table` 在 TARGET_VERIFY 路径的去向：\n- `update_batch_for_sparse` 进入 decode 分支后写入 `metadata.sparse_page_table = decode_metadata[\"sparse_page_table\"]`（一个 `zeros((2*bs, sparse_topk*block_size))`）。\n- 这个字段唯一的消费者是 `forward_extend` line 1464+ 的 sparse 分支（`flash_attn_with_block_table_v3` / `sparse_attn_with_kvcache_v3`）—— 而 TARGET_VERIFY 在 line 1462 已经 return，根本到不了。\n- 结论：`sparse_page_table` 在 verify 路径下被分配但**永不读取**，与 prefill 阶段的 block 选择结果**没有交互**。\n\n**优化潜力**：复用 prefill 的 sparse block 选择确实是一条 NSA + spec 风格的路径（matx.com/research/sd_nsa），但有这些前置：\n1. prefill 阶段的 top-K block 是基于 prefill 最后一个 query 选的；spec verify 的 query 是新的 draft tokens，分布偏移可能 miss 关键 block。\n2. 一个朴素方案：让所有 draft tokens 共享 prefill 选出的同一稀疏 mask，但需要训练侧强制对齐才不掉 accept rate。\n3. 还要先量化 dense full prefill 在 sm_120 + NVFP4 + FA2 上的实际 cost；若 dense 已经够快，做 sparse verify 的工程投入不抵收益 —— 见 §3.4。\n\n### 3.3 GLA recurrent state 在 spec 时**是否有 slot 隔离**？\n\n**结论：✅ 已经做了，而且做得很完整 —— STree 类似方案的本仓库内独立实现。**\n\n证据链（`demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/layers/attention/hybrid_linear_attn_backend.py`）：\n- Line 66-69 注释明确：*\"Eliminates 3x kernel launch overhead in TARGET_VERIFY by processing all draft_token_num steps in a single kernel call while saving per-step state for verification rollback.\"*\n- `_fused_recurrent_gla_intermediate_kernel`（line 76-186）：fused recurrent kernel，每步把 state 存到 `ht_all`（shape `(N, T_per_seq, H, K, V)`），**每个 draft token step 都有独立 state slot**。\n- Tree-aware parent retrieval（line 143-166）：`HAS_EAGLE_TREE_CUSTOM_ATTN_MASK=True` 时，按 `retrieve_parent_token` 加载该 token 在 tree 中 parent 的 state，**而非 sequential 累积** —— 正是 STree 论文 (arXiv:2505.14969) 的 tree-accumulation 思路在 GLA 上的实现。\n- `_copy_mamba_verify_state_fused`（line 455-499）：verify 结束后按每请求 `accept_length` 把对应 step 的 state copy 回主 state pool；rejected 路径的 state 不污染主 pool。\n- 接入开关：`SGLANG_EAGLE_FUSE_MAMBA_VERIFY_STATE_COPY=1`（默认开），`SGLANG_EAGLE_GLA_INDEXED_INITIAL_STATE=1`（默认开）。\n\n**正确性**：✅ 保证。tree spec 下 sibling/cousin 不互相污染，state commit 是 atomic per-request。\n\n**性能**：每 seq 6 个 state slot × 24 GLA layers × `head_dim × head_dim` ≈ ~11 MB/seq，单 GPU 84GB 可接受。\n\n**结论**：这块**没有提升空间**，已经在最优实现。\n\n### 3.4 实测：spec verify dense overhead at long ctx（2026-05-12）\n\n> 详见 §6 附录。本节只给结论。\n\n**TODO（A2 跑完回填）**：在 seq_len ∈ {8K, 32K, 64K, 128K} × dtn ∈ {7, 9, 11} 下测 verify forward 拆分耗时。\n\n---\n\n## 4. 下一步建议（按 ROI 排序）\n\n### 4.1 立刻可做\n\n**S1. 量化\"长 ctx + spec verify 的 dense overhead\"** ← 本周已开做\n- `bench/spec_verify/` 微基准\n- 测在 seq_len ∈ {8K, 32K, 64K, 128K}、dtn ∈ {7, 9, 11} 下，单次 verify forward 的 std attn 占比\n- 目的：确认 §3.1 的\"长 ctx 性能 leak\"假设是否真存在；如不存在则跳过 sparse verify\n\n### 4.2 短期（1-2 周，无须重训 draft）\n\n**S2. SpecExtend / CMR drop-in 实现**（Tier S #1）\n- 在 `eagle_worker_v2.py` 的 draft phase 之前，截取上一次 verify 的 target 末层 attn score\n- 实现 chunk-rank 选 top-k chunks，更新 draft model 的 KV cache mask\n- training-free，可直接 A/B 对比 16K+ ctx 下 accept rate\n\n**S3. SpecKV per-step γ**（Tier S #2）\n- 用 draft entropy/confidence 4 维做查表（不必上 MLP，节省 Python overhead）\n- 嵌套在现有 dynamic mode 之内：bs 决定 NO_SPEC/D5/D7，模式内 γ 抖动决定本步具体 steps\n- 与 MARS verify 完全正交\n\n### 4.3 中期（一次重训 draft）\n\n把下列**全部叠在 LK^λ baseline 上**一次性重训：\n\n**M1. VSD ARW 重采样**：用现有 LK^λ-trained draft 跑 target_regen 离线计算每条样本的平均 acceptance，作为 batch sampling weight\n**M2. Freeze lm_head**：移除 vocab remap，draft head 训练只优化 4096-dim feature，logit 完全走 target lm_head 子集\n**M3. On-policy fine-tune pass**：LK^λ 主训收敛后接一段 draft rollout + target verify 的 hard-negative fine-tune\n\n### 4.4 先**不**做\n\n- ❌ Tree shape adaptive：固定 width-2 tree 在 FP4 + sm_120 上 cost 已优\n- ❌ P-EAGLE：等 SGLang 上游合并再评估\n- ❌ Self-speculation 任何分支：sequential hybrid 已被文献证伪\n- ❌ PEARL：单卡拓扑不匹配\n\n---\n\n## 5. 关键文献索引\n\n- EAGLE-3: arXiv:2503.01840\n- MARS verify（已部署）: arXiv:2601.15498\n- SpecExtend (CMR): arXiv:2505.20776\n- SpecKV: arXiv:2605.02888\n- VSD: arXiv:2602.05774\n- LongSpec: arXiv:2502.17421\n- STree: arXiv:2505.14969\n- RASD: arXiv:2503.03434\n- P-EAGLE: arXiv:2602.01469\n- DIVERSED: arXiv:2604.07622\n- POSS: arXiv:2506.03566\n- SpecBlock: arXiv:2605.07243\n- SpecMQuant: arXiv:2505.22179\n- Aurora: arXiv:2602.06932\n- Component-Aware Self-Spec (失败案例): arXiv:2605.01106\n- NSA + Spec block-reuse: matx.com/research/sd_nsa\n\n---\n\n## 6. 附录：spec verify dense overhead 微基准（2026-05-12）\n\n> **TODO（A2 跑完回填）**\n\n设计：\n- 微基准脚本：`bench/spec_verify/bench_verify_overhead.py`\n- 目的：测 spec verify 一次 forward 在 std attn / GLA / lm_head 上的耗时拆分，覆盖 seq_len × dtn 网格\n- 端到端 e2e bench：`bench/spec_verify/run_grid.sh`\n- 数据：`bench/spec_verify/outputs/`\n\n"
}
